    {"jsonrpc":"2.0","id":3,"result":{"content":[{"text":"\"\\\"\\\"\\\"Old v1 HTTP path, retained temporarily across the upgrade.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\nfrom http.server import BaseHTTPRequestHandler, ThreadingHTTPServer\\n\\n\\nclass LegacyHandler(BaseHTTPRequestHandler):\\n    def do_POST(self):\\n        self.send_response(410)\\n        self.end_headers()\\n        self.wfile.write(b\\\"Legacy checkout retired\\\")\\n\\n\\ndef main():\\n    ThreadingHTTPServer((\\\"127.0.0.1\\\", 8080), LegacyHandler).serve_forever()\\n\"","type":"text"}],"isError":false,"structuredContent":{"result":"\"\\\"\\\"\\\"Old v1 HTTP path, retained temporarily across the upgrade.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\nfrom http.server import BaseHTTPRequestHandler, ThreadingHTTPServer\\n\\n\\nclass LegacyHandler(BaseHTTPRequestHandler):\\n    def do_POST(self):\\n        self.send_response(410)\\n        self.end_headers()\\n        self.wfile.write(b\\\"Legacy checkout retired\\\")\\n\\n\\ndef main():\\n    ThreadingHTTPServer((\\\"127.0.0.1\\\", 8080), LegacyHandler).serve_forever()\\n\""}}}

    {"jsonrpc":"2.0","id":3,"result":{"content":[{"text":"\"\\\"\\\"\\\"Current checkout behavior.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n\\n\\ndef create_checkout(cart, method=\\\"card\\\"):\\n    items = cart.get(\\\"items\\\", [])\\n    if not items or any(item.get(\\\"quantity\\\", 0) < 1 for item in items):\\n        raise ValueError(\\\"The cart needs items with positive quantities\\\")\\n    if method not in (\\\"card\\\", \\\"bank_transfer\\\"):\\n        raise ValueError(\\\"Unsupported payment method\\\")\\n    cents = sum(item[\\\"price_cents\\\"] * item[\\\"quantity\\\"] for item in items)\\n    return {\\\"status\\\": \\\"pending_payment\\\", \\\"amount_cents\\\": cents, \\\"method\\\": method}\\n\"","type":"text"}],"isError":false,"structuredContent":{"result":"\"\\\"\\\"\\\"Current checkout behavior.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n\\n\\ndef create_checkout(cart, method=\\\"card\\\"):\\n    items = cart.get(\\\"items\\\", [])\\n    if not items or any(item.get(\\\"quantity\\\", 0) < 1 for item in items):\\n        raise ValueError(\\\"The cart needs items with positive quantities\\\")\\n    if method not in (\\\"card\\\", \\\"bank_transfer\\\"):\\n        raise ValueError(\\\"Unsupported payment method\\\")\\n    cents = sum(item[\\\"price_cents\\\"] * item[\\\"quantity\\\"] for item in items)\\n    return {\\\"status\\\": \\\"pending_payment\\\", \\\"amount_cents\\\": cents, \\\"method\\\": method}\\n\""}}}

Handler(BaseHTTPRequestHandler):\\n    def do_GET(self):\\n        if self.path == \\\"/api/status\\\":\\n            return self.reply(200, {\\\"service\\\": \\\"checkout\\\", \\\"version\\\": \\\"2.0\\\"})\\n        if self.path in (\\\"/\\\", \\\"/app.js\\\"):\\n            name = \\\"index.html\\\" if self.path == \\\"/\\\" else \\\"app.js\\\"\\n            body = (WEB_ROOT / name).read_bytes()\\n            self.send_response(200)\\n            self.send_header(\\\"Content-Type\\\", \\\"text/html\\\" if name.endswith(\\\"html\\\") else \\\"text/javascript\\\")\\n            self.send_header(\\\"Content-Length\\\", str(len(body)))\\n            self.end_headers()\\n            self.wfile.write(body)\\n            return\\n        self.reply(404, {\\\"error\\\": \\\"Not found\\\"})\\n\\n    def do_POST(self):\\n        if self.path != \\\"/api/checkout\\\":\\n            return self.reply(404, {\\\"error\\\": \\\"Not found\\\"})\\n        try:\\n            size = int(self.headers.get(\\\"Content-Length\\\", \\\"0\\\"))\\n            if size > 65536:\\n                raise ValueError(\\\"Request too large\\\")\\n            payload = json.loads(self.rfile.read(size))\\n            result = create_checkout(payload[\\\"cart\\\"], payload.get(\\\"method\\\", \\\"card\\\"))\\n            self.reply(200, result)\\n        except (KeyError, ValueError, TypeError, json.JSONDecodeError) as exc:\\n            self.reply(400, {\\\"error\\\": str(exc)})\\n\\n    def reply(self, status, value):\\n        body = \\\"__main__\\\":\\n    main()\\n\"","type":"text"}],"isError":false,"structuredContent":{"result":"\"\\\"\\\"\\\"Standard-library HTTP entry point; no external package installation.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\nimport json\\nfrom http.server import BaseHTTPRequestHandler, ThreadingHTTPServer\\nfrom pathlib import Path\\nfrom .checkout import create_checkout\\n\\nWEB_ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent / \\\"web\\\"\\n\\n\\nclass Handler(BaseHTTPRequestHandler):\\n    def do_GET(self):\\n        if self.path == \\\"/api/status\\\":\\n            return self.reply(200, {\\\"service\\\": \\\"checkout\\\", \\\"version\\\": \\\"2.0\\\"})\\n        if self.path in (\\\"/\\\", \\\"/app.js\\\"):\\n            name = \\\"index.html\\\" if self.path == \\\"/\\\" else \\\"app.js\\\"\\n            body = (WEB_ROOT / name).read_bytes()\\n            self.send_response(200)\\n            self.send_header(\\\"Content-Type\\\", \\\"text/html\\\" if name.endswith(\\\"html\\\") else \\\"text/javascript\\\")\\n            self.send_header(\\\"Content-Length\\\", str(len(body)))\\n            self.end_headers()\\n            self.wfile.write(body)\\n            return\\n        self.reply(404, {\\\"error\\\": \\\"Not found\\\"})\\n\\n    def do_POST(self):\\n        if self.path != \\\"/api/checkout\\\":\\n            return self.reply(404, {\\\"error\\\": \\\"Not found\\\"})\\n        try:\\n            size = int(self.headers.get(\\\"Content-Length\\\", \\\"0\\\"))\\n            if size > 65536:\\n                raise ValueError(\\\"Request too large\\\")\\n            payload = json.loads(self.rfile.read(size))\\n            result = create_checkout(payload[\\\"cart\\\"], payload.get(\\\"method\\\", \\\"card\\\"))\\n            self.reply(200, result)\\n        except (KeyError, ValueError, TypeError, json.JSONDecodeError) as exc:\\n            self.reply(400, {\\\"error\\\": str(exc)})\\n\\n    def reply(self, status, value):\\n        body = json.dumps(value).encode()\\n        self.send_response(status)\\n        self.send_header(\\\"Content-Type\\\", \\\"application/json\\\")\\n        self.send_header(\\\"Content-Length\\\", str(len(body)))\\n        self.end_headers()\\n        self.wfile.write(body)\\n\\n\\ndef main():\\n    ThreadingHTTPServer((\\\"127.0.0.1\\\", 8080), Handler).serve_forever()\\n\\n\\nif __name__ == \\\"__main__\\\":\\n    main()\\n\""}}}

    {"jsonrpc":"2.0","id":3,"result":{"content":[{"text":"\"# Checkout sample\\n\\nThis small service exposes `GET /api/status` and `POST /api/checkout` on port 8080.\\nStart it with `python -m checkout_app.server`, then open `web/index.html` through\\nthe same server. Run `python -m unittest discover -s tests` to check the API.\\n\\n`versions/before.json` and `versions/after.json` identify the upgrade; the\\nprior service snapshot is runnable via `python versions/before/server.py` on\\nport 8081. The current executable path is selected in `run.py`.\\n\"","type":"text"}],"isError":false,"structuredContent":{"result":"\"# Checkout sample\\n\\nThis small service exposes `GET /api/status` and `POST /api/checkout` on port 8080.\\nStart it with `python -m checkout_app.server`, then open `web/index.html` through\\nthe same server. Run `python -m unittest discover -s tests` to check the API.\\n\\n`versions/before.json` and `versions/after.json` identify the upgrade; the\\nprior service snapshot is runnable via `python versions/before/server.py` on\\nport 8081. The current executable path is selected in `run.py`.\\n\""}}}

def reply(self, status, value):\\n        body = json.dumps(value).encode()\\n        self.send_response(status)\\n        self.send_header(\\\"Content-Type\\\", \\\"application/json\\\")\\n     {"jsonrpc":"2.0","id":3,"result":{"content":[{"text":"\"# Checkout sample\\n\\nThis small service exposes `GET /api/status` and `POST /api/checkout` on port 8080.\\nStart it with `python -m checkout_app.server`, then open `web/index.html` through\\nthe same server. Run `python -m unittest discover -s tests` to check the API.\\n\\n`versions/before.json` and `versions/after.json` identify the upgrade; the\\nprior service snapshot is runnable via `python versions/before/server.py` on\\nport 8081. The current executable path is selected in `run.py`.\\n\"","type":"text"}],"isError":false,"structuredContent":{"result":"\"# Checkout sample\\n\\nThis small service exposes `GET /api/status` and `POST /api/checkout` on port 8080.\\nStart it with `python -m checkout_app.server`, then open `web/index.html` through\\nthe same server. Run `python -m unittest discover -s tests` to check the API.\\n\\n`versions/before.json` and `versions/after.json` identify the upgrade; the\\nprior service snapshot is runnable via `python versions/before/server.py` on\\nport 8081. The current executable path is selected in `run.py`.\\n\""}}}

    {"jsonrpc":"2.0","id":3,"result":{"content":[{"text":"\"import unittest\\nfrom checkout_app.checkout import create_checkout\\n\\n\\nclass CheckoutTests(unittest.TestCase):\\n    def test_card_checkout(self):\\n        self.assertEqual(create_checkout({\\\"items\\\": [{\\\"price_cents\\\": 1250, \\\"quantity\\\": 2}]}),\\n                         {\\\"status\\\": \\\"pending_payment\\\", \\\"amount_cents\\\": 2500, \\\"method\\\": \\\"card\\\"})\\n\\n    def test_empty_cart(self):\\n        with self.assertRaises(ValueError):\\n            create_checkout({\\\"items\\\": []})\\n\\n\\nif __name__ == \\\"__main__\\\":\\n    unittest.main()\\n\"","type":"text"}],"isError":false,"structuredContent":{"result":"\"import unittest\\nfrom checkout_app.checkout import create_checkout\\n\\n\\nclass CheckoutTests(unittest.TestCase):\\n    def test_card_checkout(self):\\n        self.assertEqual(create_checkout({\\\"items\\\": [{\\\"price_cents\\\": 1250, \\\"quantity\\\": 2}]}),\\n                         {\\\"status\\\": \\\"pending_payment\\\", \\\"amount_cents\\\": 2500, \\\"method\\\": \\\"card\\\"})\\n\\n    def test_empty_cart(self):\\n        with self.assertRaises(ValueError):\\n            create_checkout({\\\"items\\\": []})\\n\\n\\nif __name__ == \\\"__main__\\\":\\n    unittest.main()\\n\""}}}

    {"jsonrpc":"2.0","id":3,"result":{"content":[{"text":"\"# Checkout sample\\n\\nThis small service exposes `GET /api/status` and `POST /api/checkout` on port 8080.\\nStart it with `python -m checkout_app.server`, then open `web/index.html` through\\nthe same server. Run `python -m unittest discover -s tests` to check the API.\\n\\n`versions/before.json` and `versions/after.json` identify the upgrade; the\\nprior service snapshot is runnable via `python versions/before/server.py` on\\nport 8081. The current executable path is selected in `run.py`.\\n\"","type":"text"}],"isError":false,"structuredContent":{"result":"\"# Checkout sample\\n\\nThis small service exposes `GET /api/status` and `POST /api/checkout` on port 8080.\\nStart it with `python -m checkout_app.server`, then open `web/index.html` through\\nthe same server. Run `python -m unittest discover -s tests` to check the API.\\n\\n`versions/before.json` and `versions/after.json` identify the upgrade; the\\nprior service snapshot is runnable via `python versions/before/server.py` on\\nport 8081. The current executable path is selected in `run.py`.\\n\""}}}

    {"jsonrpc":"2.0","id":3,"result":{"content":[{"text":"\"import unittest\\nfrom checkout_app.checkout import create_checkout\\n\\n\\nclass CheckoutTests(unittest.TestCase):\\n    def test_card_checkout(self):\\n        self.assertEqual(create_checkout({\\\"items\\\": [{\\\"price_cents\\\": 1250, \\\"quantity\\\": 2}]}),\\n                         {\\\"status\\\": \\\"pending_payment\\\", \\\"amount_cents\\\": 2500, \\\"method\\\": \\\"card\\\"})\\n\\n    def test_empty_cart(self):\\n        with self.assertRaises(ValueError):\\n            create_checkout({\\\"items\\\": []})\\n\\n\\nif __name__ == \\\"__main__\\\":\\n    unittest.main()\\n\"","type":"text"}],"isError":false,"structuredContent":{"result":"\"import unittest\\nfrom checkout_app.checkout import create_checkout\\n\\n\\nclass CheckoutTests(unittest.TestCase):\\n    def test_card_checkout(self):\\n        self.assertEqual(create_checkout({\\\"items\\\": [{\\\"price_cents\\\": 1250, \\\"quantity\\\": 2}]}),\\n                         {\\\"status\\\": \\\"pending_payment\\\", \\\"amount_cents\\\": 2500, \\\"method\\\": \\\"card\\\"})\\n\\n    def test_empty_cart(self):\\n        with self.assertRaises(ValueError):\\n            create_checkout({\\\"items\\\": []})\\n\\n\\nif __name__ == \\\"__main__\\\":\\n    unittest.main()\\n\""}}}

    {"jsonrpc":"2.0","id":3,"result":{"content":[{"text":"\"# Checkout sample\\n\\nThis small service exposes `GET /api/status` and `POST /api/checkout` on port 8080.\\nStart it with `python -m checkout_app.server`, then open `web/index.html` through\\nthe same server. Run `python -m unittest discover -s tests` to check the API.\\n\\n`versions/before.json` and `versions/after.json` identify the upgrade; the\\nprior service snapshot is runnable via `python versions/before/server.py` on\\nport 8081. The current executable path is selected in `run.py`.\\n\"","type":"text"}],"isError":false,"structuredContent":{"result":"\"# Checkout sample\\n\\nThis small service exposes `GET /api/status` and `POST /api/checkout` on port 8080.\\nStart it with `python -m checkout_app.server`, then open `web/index.html` through\\nthe same server. Run `python -m unittest discover -s tests` to check the API.\\n\\n`versions/before.json` and `versions/after.json` identify the upgrade; the\\nprior service snapshot is runnable via `python versions/before/server.py` on\\nport 8081. The current executable path is selected in `run.py`.\\n\""}}}

    {"jsonrpc":"2.0","id":3,"result":{"content":[{"text":"\"import unittest\\nfrom checkout_app.checkout import create_checkout\\n\\n\\nclass CheckoutTests(unittest.TestCase):\\n    def test_card_checkout(self):\\n        self.assertEqual(create_checkout({\\\"items\\\": [{\\\"price_cents\\\": 1250, \\\"quantity\\\": 2}]}),\\n                         {\\\"status\\\": \\\"pending_payment\\\", \\\"amount_cents\\\": 2500, \\\"method\\\": \\\"card\\\"})\\n\\n    def test_empty_cart(self):\\n        with self.assertRaises(ValueError):\\n            create_checkout({\\\"items\\\": []})\\n\\n\\nif __name__ == \\\"__main__\\\":\\n    unittest.main()\\n\"","type":"text"}],"isError":false,"structuredContent":{"result":"\"import unittest\\nfrom checkout_app.checkout import create_checkout\\n\\n\\nclass CheckoutTests(unittest.TestCase):\\n    def test_card_checkout(self):\\n        self.assertEqual(create_checkout({\\\"items\\\": [{\\\"price_cents\\\": 1250, \\\"quantity\\\": 2}]}),\\n                         {\\\"status\\\": \\\"pending_payment\\\", \\\"amount_cents\\\": 2500, \\\"method\\\": \\\"card\\\"})\\n\\n    def test_empty_cart(self):\\n        with self.assertRaises(ValueError):\\n            create_checkout({\\\"items\\\": []})\\n\\n\\nif __name__ == \\\"__main__\\\":\\n    unittest.main()\\n\""}}}

    {"jsonrpc":"2.0","id":3,"result":{"content":[{"text":"\"# Checkout sample\\n\\nThis small service exposes `GET /api/status` and `POST /api/checkout` on port 8080.\\nStart it with `python -m checkout_app.server`, then open `web/index.html` through\\nthe same server. Run `python -m unittest discover -s tests` to check the API.\\n\\n`versions/before.json` and `versions/after.json` identify the upgrade; the\\nprior service snapshot is runnable via `python versions/before/server.py` on\\nport 8081. The current executable path is selected in `run.py`.\\n\"","type":"text"}],"isError":false,"structuredContent":{"result":"\"# Checkout sample\\n\\nThis small service exposes `GET /api/status` and `POST /api/checkout` on port 8080.\\nStart it with `python -m checkout_app.server`, then open `web/index.html` through\\nthe same server. Run `python -m unittest discover -s tests` to check the API.\\n\\n`versions/before.json` and `versions/after.json` identify the upgrade; the\\nprior service snapshot is runnable via `python versions/before/server.py` on\\nport 8081. The current executable path is selected in `run.py`.\\n\""}}}

    {"jsonrpc":"2.0","id":3,"result":{"content":[{"text":"\"\\\"\\\"\\\"Runnable checkout v1.9 snapshot from before the upgrade.\\n\\nRun `python versions/before/server.py` in the project root. This older service\\nlistens on 8081 so its behavior can be compared with the new service on 8080.\\n\\\"\\\"\\\"\\nimport json\\nfrom http.server import BaseHTTPRequestHandler, ThreadingHTTPServer\\n\\n\\nclass Handler(BaseHTTPRequestHandler):\\n    def do_GET(self):\\n        if self.path == \\\"/api/status\\\":\\n            return self.reply(200, {\\\"service\\\": \\\"checkout\\\", \\\"version\\\": \\\"1.9\\\"})\\n        self.reply(404, {\\\"error\\\": \\\"Not found\\\"})\\n\\n    def do_POST(self):\\n        if self.path != \\\"/api/checkout\\\":\\n            return self.reply(404, {\\\"error\\\": \\\"Not found\\\"})\\n        try:\\n            payload = json.loads(self.rfile.read(int(self.headers.get(\\\"Content-Length\\\", \\\"0\\\"))))\\n            items = payload[\\\"cart\\\"][\\\"items\\\"]\\n            if not items:\\n                raise ValueError(\\\"Cart is empty\\\")\\n            amount = sum(item[\\\"price_cents\\\"] * item[\\\"quantity\\\"] for item in items)\\n            self.reply(200, {\\\"status\\\": \\\"pending_payment\\\", \\\"amount_cents\\\": amount, \\\"method\\\": \\\"card\\\"})\\n        except (KeyError, ValueError, TypeError) as exc:\\n            self.reply(400, {\\\"error\\\": str(exc)})\\n\\n    def reply(self, status, value):\\n        body = json.dumps(value).encode()\\n        self.send_response(status)\\n        self.send_header(\\\"Content-Type\\\", \\\"application/json\\\")\\n        self.send_header(\\\"Content-Length\\\", str(len(body)))\\n        self.end_headers()\\n        self.wfile.write(body)\\n\\n\\nif __name__ == \\\"__main__\\\":\\n    ThreadingHTTPServer((\\\"127.0.0.1\\\", 8081), Handler).serve_forever()\\n\"","type":"text"}],"isError":false,"structuredContent":{"result":"\"\\\"\\\"\\\"Runnable checkout v1.9 snapshot from before the upgrade.\\n\\nRun `python versions/before/server.py` in the project root. This older service\\nlistens on 8081 so its behavior can be compared with the new service on 8080.\\n\\\"\\\"\\\"\\nimport json\\nfrom http.server import BaseHTTPRequestHandler, ThreadingHTTPServer\\n\\n\\nclass Handler(BaseHTTPRequestHandler):\\n    def do_GET(self):\\n        if self.path == \\\"/api/status\\\":\\n            return self.reply(200, {\\\"service\\\": \\\"checkout\\\", \\\"version\\\": \\\"1.9\\\"})\\n        self.reply(404, {\\\"error\\\": \\\"Not found\\\"})\\n\\n    def do_POST(self):\\n        if self.path != \\\"/api/checkout\\\":\\n            return self.reply(404, {\\\"error\\\": \\\"Not found\\\"})\\n        try:\\n            payload = json.loads(self.rfile.read(int(self.headers.get(\\\"Content-Length\\\", \\\"0\\\"))))\\n            items = payload[\\\"cart\\\"][\\\"items\\\"]\\n            if not items:\\n                raise ValueError(\\\"Cart is empty\\\")\\n            amount = sum(item[\\\"price_cents\\\"] * item[\\\"quantity\\\"] for item in items)\\n            self.reply(200, {\\\"status\\\": \\\"pending_payment\\\", \\\"amount_cents\\\": amount, \\\"method\\\": \\\"card\\\"})\\n        except (KeyError, ValueError, TypeError) as exc:\\n            self.reply(400, {\\\"error\\\": str(exc)})\\n\\n    def reply(self, status, value):\\n        body = json.dumps(value).encode()\\n        self.send_response(status)\\n        self.send_header(\\\"Content-Type\\\", \\\"application/json\\\")\\n        self.send_header(\\\"Content-Length\\\", str(len(body)))\\n        self.end_headers()\\n        self.wfile.write(body)\\n\\n\\nif __name__ == \\\"__main__\\\":\\n    ThreadingHTTPServer((\\\"127.0.0.1\\\", 8081), Handler).serve_forever()\\n\""}}}

    {"jsonrpc":"2.0","id":3,"result":{"content":[{"text":"\"\\\"\\\"\\\"Current checkout behavior.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n\\n\\ndef create_checkout(cart, method=\\\"card\\\"):\\n    items = cart.get(\\\"items\\\", [])\\n    if not items or any(item.get(\\\"quantity\\\", 0) < 1 for item in items):\\n        raise ValueError(\\\"The cart needs items with positive quantities\\\")\\n    if method not in (\\\"card\\\", \\\"bank_transfer\\\"):\\n        raise ValueError(\\\"Unsupported payment method\\\")\\n    cents = sum(item[\\\"price_cents\\\"] * item[\\\"quantity\\\"] for item in items)\\n    return {\\\"status\\\": \\\"pending_payment\\\", \\\"amount_cents\\\": cents, \\\"method\\\": method}\\n\"","type":"text"}],"isError":false,"structuredContent":{"result":"\"\\\"\\\"\\\"Current checkout behavior.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n\\n\\ndef create_checkout(cart, method=\\\"card\\\"):\\n    items = cart.get(\\\"items\\\", [])\\n    if not items or any(item.get(\\\"quantity\\\", 0) < 1 for item in items):\\n        raise ValueError(\\\"The cart needs items with positive quantities\\\")\\n    if method not in (\\\"card\\\", \\\"bank_transfer\\\"):\\n        raise ValueError(\\\"Unsupported payment method\\\")\\n    cents = sum(item[\\\"price_cents\\\"] * item[\\\"quantity\\\"] for item in items)\\n    return {\\\"status\\\": \\\"pending_payment\\\", \\\"amount_cents\\\": cents, \\\"method\\\": method}\\n\""}}}

    {"jsonrpc":"2.0","id":3,"result":{"content":[{"text":"\"\\\"\\\"\\\"Runnable checkout v1.9 snapshot from before the upgrade.\\n\\nRun `python versions/before/server.py` in the project root. This older service\\nlistens on 8081 so its behavior can be compared with the new service on 8080.\\n\\\"\\\"\\\"\\nimport json\\nfrom http.server import BaseHTTPRequestHandler, ThreadingHTTPServer\\n\\n\\nclass Handler(BaseHTTPRequestHandler):\\n    def do_GET(self):\\n        if self.path == \\\"/api/status\\\":\\n            return self.reply(200, {\\\"service\\\": \\\"checkout\\\", \\\"version\\\": \\\"1.9\\\"})\\n        self.reply(404, {\\\"error\\\": \\\"Not found\\\"})\\n\\n    def do_POST(self):\\n        if self.path != \\\"/api/checkout\\\":\\n            return self.reply(404, {\\\"error\\\": \\\"Not found\\\"})\\n        try:\\n            payload = json.loads(self.rfile.read(int(self.headers.get(\\\"Content-Length\\\", \\\"0\\\"))))\\n            items = payload[\\\"cart\\\"][\\\"items\\\"]\\n            if not items:\\n                raise ValueError(\\\"Cart is empty\\\")\\n            amount = sum(item[\\\"price_cents\\\"] * item[\\\"quantity\\\"] for item in items)\\n            self.reply(200, {\\\"status\\\": \\\"pending_payment\\\", \\\"amount_cents\\\": amount, \\\"method\\\": \\\"card\\\"})\\n        except (KeyError, ValueError, TypeError) as exc:\\n            self.reply(400, {\\\"error\\\": str(exc)})\\n\\n    def reply(self, status, value):\\n        body = json.dumps(value).encode()\\n        self.send_response(status)\\n        self.send_header(\\\"Content-Type\\\", \\\"application/json\\\")\\n        self.send_header(\\\"Content-Length\\\", str(len(body)))\\n        self.end_headers()\\n        self.wfile.write(body)\\n\\n\\nif __name__ == \\\"__main__\\\":\\n    ThreadingHTTPServer((\\\"127.0.0.1\\\", 8081), Handler).serve_forever()\\n\"","type":"text"}],"isError":false,"structuredContent":{"result":"\"\\\"\\\"\\\"Runnable checkout v1.9 snapshot from before the upgrade.\\n\\nRun `python versions/before/server.py` in the project root. This older service\\nlistens on 8081 so its behavior can be compared with the new service on 8080.\\n\\\"\\\"\\\"\\nimport json\\nfrom http.server import BaseHTTPRequestHandler, ThreadingHTTPServer\\n\\n\\nclass Handler(BaseHTTPRequestHandler):\\n    def do_GET(self):\\n        if self.path == \\\"/api/status\\\":\\n            return self.reply(200, {\\\"service\\\": \\\"checkout\\\", \\\"version\\\": \\\"1.9\\\"})\\n        self.reply(404, {\\\"error\\\": \\\"Not found\\\"})\\n\\n    def do_POST(self):\\n        if self.path != \\\"/api/checkout\\\":\\n            return self.reply(404, {\\\"error\\\": \\\"Not found\\\"})\\n        try:\\n            payload = json.loads(self.rfile.read(int(self.headers.get(\\\"Content-Length\\\", \\\"0\\\"))))\\n            items = payload[\\\"cart\\\"][\\\"items\\\"]\\n            if not items:\\n                raise ValueError(\\\"Cart is empty\\\")\\n            amount = sum(item[\\\"price_cents\\\"] * item[\\\"quantity\\\"] for item in items)\\n            self.reply(200, {\\\"status\\\": \\\"pending_payment\\\", \\\"amount_cents\\\": amount, \\\"method\\\": \\\"card\\\"})\\n        except (KeyError, ValueError, TypeError) as exc:\\n            self.reply(400, {\\\"error\\\": str(exc)})\\n\\n    def reply(self, status, value):\\n        body = json.dumps(value).encode()\\n        self.send_response(status)\\n        self.send_header(\\\"Content-Type\\\", \\\"application/json\\\")\\n        self.send_header(\\\"Content-Length\\\", str(len(body)))\\n        self.end_headers()\\n        self.wfile.write(body)\\n\\n\\nif __name__ == \\\"__main__\\\":\\n    ThreadingHTTPServer((\\\"127.0.0.1\\\", 8081), Handler).serve_forever()\\n\""}}}

    {"jsonrpc":"2.0","id":3,"result":{"content":[{"text":"\"\\\"\\\"\\\"Current checkout behavior.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n\\n\\ndef create_checkout(cart, method=\\\"card\\\"):\\n    items = cart.get(\\\"items\\\", [])\\n    if not items or any(item.get(\\\"quantity\\\", 0) < 1 for item in items):\\n        raise ValueError(\\\"The cart needs items with positive quantities\\\")\\n    if method not in (\\\"card\\\", \\\"bank_transfer\\\"):\\n        raise ValueError(\\\"Unsupported payment method\\\")\\n    cents = sum(item[\\\"price_cents\\\"] * item[\\\"quantity\\\"] for item in items)\\n    return {\\\"status\\\": \\\"pending_payment\\\", \\\"amount_cents\\\": cents, \\\"method\\\": method}\\n\"","type":"text"}],"isError":false,"structuredContent":{"result":"\"\\\"\\\"\\\"Current checkout behavior.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n\\n\\ndef create_checkout(cart, method=\\\"card\\\"):\\n    items = cart.get(\\\"items\\\", [])\\n    if not items or any(item.get(\\\"quantity\\\", 0) < 1 for item in items):\\n        raise ValueError(\\\"The cart needs items with positive quantities\\\")\\n    if method not in (\\\"card\\\", \\\"bank_transfer\\\"):\\n        raise ValueError(\\\"Unsupported payment method\\\")\\n    cents = sum(item[\\\"price_cents\\\"] * item[\\\"quantity\\\"] for item in items)\\n    return {\\\"status\\\": \\\"pending_payment\\\", \\\"amount_cents\\\": cents, \\\"method\\\": method}\\n\""}}}

Run `python -m unittest discover -s tests` to check the API.\\n\\n`versions/before.json` and `versions/after.json` identify the upgrade; the\\nprior service snapshot is runnable via `python versions/before/server.py` on\\nport 8081. The current executable path is selected in `run.py`.\\n\"","type":"text"}],"isError":false,"structuredContent":{"result":"\"# Checkout sample\\n\\nThis small service exposes `GET /api/status` and `POST /api/checkout` on port 8080.\\nStart it with `python -m checkout_app.server`, then open `web/index.html` through\\nthe same server. Run `python -m unittest discover -s tests` to check the API.\\n\\n`versions/before.json` and `versions/after.json` identify the upgrade; the\\nprior service snapshot is runnable via `python versions/before/server.py` on\\nport 8081. The current executable path is selected in `run.py`.\\n\""}}}

    {"jsonrpc":"2.0","id":3,"result":{"content":[{"text":"\"\\\"\\\"\\\"Standard-library HTTP entry point; no external package installation.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\nimport json\\nfrom http.server import BaseHTTPRequestHandler, ThreadingHTTPServer\\nfrom pathlib import Path\\nfrom .checkout import create_checkout\\n\\nWEB_ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent / \\\"web\\\"\\n\\n\\nclass Handler(BaseHTTPRequestHandler):\\n    def do_GET(self):\\n        if self.path == \\\"/api/status\\\":\\n            return self.reply(200, {\\\"service\\\": \\\"checkout\\\", \\\"version\\\": \\\"2.0\\\"})\\n        if self.path in (\\\"/\\\", \\\"/app.js\\\"):\\n            name = \\\"index.html\\\" if self.path == \\\"/\\\" else \\\"app.js\\\"\\n            body = (WEB_ROOT / name).read_bytes()\\n            self.send_response(200)\\n            self.send_header(\\\"Content-Type\\\", \\\"text/html\\\" if name.endswith(\\\"html\\\") else \\\"text/javascript\\\")\\n            self.send_header(\\\"Content-Length\\\", str(len(body)))\\n            self.end_headers()\\n            self.wfile.write(body)\\n            return\\n        self.reply(404, {\\\"error\\\": \\\"Not found\\\"})\\n\\n    def do_POST(self):\\n        if self.path != \\\"/api/checkout\\\":\\n            return self.reply(404, {\\\"error\\\": \\\"Not found\\\"})\\n        try:\\n            size = int(self.headers.get(\\\"Content-Length\\\", \\\"0\\\"))\\n            if size > 65536:\\n                raise ValueError(\\\"Request too large\\\")\\n            payload =     {"jsonrpc":"2.0","id":3,"result":{"content":[{"text":"\"import unittest\\nfrom checkout_app.checkout import create_checkout\\n\\n\\nclass CheckoutTests(unittest.TestCase):\\n    def test_card_checkout(self):\\n        self.assertEqual(create_checkout({\\\"items\\\": [{\\\"price_cents\\\": 1250, \\\"quantity\\\": 2}]}),\\n                         {\\\"status\\\": \\\"pending_payment\\\", \\\"amount_cents\\\": 2500, \\\"method\\\": \\\"card\\\"})\\n\\n    def test_empty_cart(self):\\n        with self.assertRaises(ValueError):\\n            create_checkout({\\\"items\\\": []})\\n\\n\\nif __name__ == \\\"__main__\\\":\\n    unittest.main()\\n\"","type":"text"}],"isError":false,"structuredContent":{"result":"\"import unittest\\nfrom checkout_app.checkout import create_checkout\\n\\n\\nclass CheckoutTests(unittest.TestCase):\\n    def test_card_checkout(self):\\n        self.assertEqual(create_checkout({\\\"items\\\": [{\\\"price_cents\\\": 1250, \\\"quantity\\\": 2}]}),\\n                         {\\\"status\\\": \\\"pending_payment\\\", \\\"amount_cents\\\": 2500, \\\"method\\\": \\\"card\\\"})\\n\\n    def test_empty_cart(self):\\n        with self.assertRaises(ValueError):\\n            create_checkout({\\\"items\\\": []})\\n\\n\\nif __name__ == \\\"__main__\\\":\\n    unittest.main()\\n\""}}}

    {"jsonrpc":"2.0","id":3,"result":{"content":[{"text":"\"# Checkout sample\\n\\nThis small service exposes `GET /api/status` and `POST /api/checkout` on port 8080.\\nStart it with `python -m checkout_app.server`, then open `web/index.html` through\\nthe same server. Run `python -m unittest discover -s tests` to check the API.\\n\\n`versions/before.json` and `versions/after.json` identify the upgrade; the\\nprior service snapshot is runnable via `python versions/before/server.py` on\\nport 8081. The current executable path is selected in `run.py`.\\n\"","type":"text"}],"isError":false,"structuredContent":{"result":"\"# Checkout sample\\n\\nThis small service exposes `GET /api/status` and `POST /api/checkout` on port 8080.\\nStart it with `python -m checkout_app.server`, then open `web/index.html` through\\nthe same server. Run `python -m unittest discover -s tests` to check the API.\\n\\n`versions/before.json` and `versions/after.json` identify the upgrade; the\\nprior service snapshot is runnable via `python versions/before/server.py` on\\nport 8081. The current executable path is selected in `run.py`.\\n\""}}}

    {"jsonrpc":"2.0","id":3,"result":{"content":[{"text":"\"\\\"\\\"\\\"Current checkout behavior.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n\\n\\ndef create_checkout(cart, method=\\\"card\\\"):\\n    items = cart.get(\\\"items\\\", [])\\n    if not items or any(item.get(\\\"quantity\\\", 0) < 1 for item in items):\\n        raise ValueError(\\\"The cart needs items with positive quantities\\\")\\n    if method not in (\\\"card\\\", \\\"bank_transfer\\\"):\\n        raise ValueError(\\\"Unsupported payment method\\\")\\n    cents = sum(item[\\\"price_cents\\\"] * item[\\\"quantity\\\"] for item in items)\\n    return {\\\"status\\\": \\\"pending_payment\\\", \\\"amount_cents\\\": cents, \\\"method\\\": method}\\n\"","type":"text"}],"isError":false,"structuredContent":{"result":"\"\\\"\\\"\\\"Current checkout behavior.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n\\n\\ndef create_checkout(cart, method=\\\"card\\\"):\\n    items = cart.get(\\\"items\\\", [])\\n    if not items or any(item.get(\\\"quantity\\\", 0) < 1 for item in items):\\n        raise ValueError(\\\"The cart needs items with positive quantities\\\")\\n    if method not in (\\\"card\\\", \\\"bank_transfer\\\"):\\n        raise ValueError(\\\"Unsupported payment method\\\")\\n    cents = sum(item[\\\"price_cents\\\"] * item[\\\"quantity\\\"] for item in items)\\n    return {\\\"status\\\": \\\"pending_payment\\\", \\\"amount_cents\\\": cents, \\\"method\\\": method}\\n\""}}}

    {"jsonrpc":"2.0","id":3,"result":{"content":[{"text":"\"# Checkout sample\\n\\nThis small service exposes `GET /api/status` and `POST /api/checkout` on port 8080.\\nStart it with `python -m checkout_app.server`, then open `web/index.html` through\\nthe same server. Run `python -m unittest discover -s tests` to check the API.\\n\\n`versions/before.json` and `versions/after.json` identify the upgrade; the\\nprior service snapshot is runnable via `python versions/before/server.py` on\\nport 8081. The current executable path is selected in `run.py`.\\n\"","type":"text"}],"isError":false,"structuredContent":{"result":"\"# Checkout sample\\n\\nThis small service exposes `GET /api/status` and `POST /api/checkout` on port 8080.\\nStart it with `python -m checkout_app.server`, then open `web/index.html` through\\nthe same server. Run `python -m unittest discover -s tests` to check the API.\\n\\n`versions/before.json` and `versions/after.json` identify the upgrade; the\\nprior service snapshot is runnable via `python versions/before/server.py` on\\nport 8081. The current executable path is selected in `run.py`.\\n\""}}}

    {"jsonrpc":"2.0","id":3,"result":{"content":[{"text":"\"import unittest\\nfrom checkout_app.checkout import create_checkout\\n\\n\\nclass CheckoutTests(unittest.TestCase):\\n    def test_card_checkout(self):\\n        self.assertEqual(create_checkout({\\\"items\\\": [{\\\"price_cents\\\": 1250, \\\"quantity\\\": 2}]}),\\n                         {\\\"status\\\": \\\"pending_payment\\\", \\\"amount_cents\\\": 2500, \\\"method\\\": \\\"card\\\"})\\n\\n    def test_empty_cart(self):\\n        with self.assertRaises(ValueError):\\n            create_checkout({\\\"items\\\": []})\\n\\n\\nif __name__ == \\\"__main__\\\":\\n    unittest.main()\\n\"","type":"text"}],"isError":false,"structuredContent":{"result":"\"import unittest\\nfrom checkout_app.checkout import create_checkout\\n\\n\\nclass CheckoutTests(unittest.TestCase):\\n    def test_card_checkout(self):\\n        self.assertEqual(create_checkout({\\\"items\\\": [{\\\"price_cents\\\": 1250, \\\"quantity\\\": 2}]}),\\n                         {\\\"status\\\": \\\"pending_payment\\\", \\\"amount_cents\\\": 2500, \\\"method\\\": \\\"card\\\"})\\n\\n    def test_empty_cart(self):\\n        with self.assertRaises(ValueError):\\n            create_checkout({\\\"items\\\": []})\\n\\n\\nif __name__ == \\\"__main__\\\":\\n    unittest.main()\\n\""}}}

### README.md | sha256=11fe60236b048a9794f3441196c886f2ba9c52cf0bfd3dff95975a908aa99f12
    # Checkout sample
    
    This small service exposes `GET /api/status` and `POST /api/checkout` on port 8080.
    Start it with `python -m checkout_app.server`, then open `web/index.html` through
    the same server. Run `python -m unittest discover -s tests` to check the API.
    
    `versions/before.json` and `versions/after.json` identify the upgrade; the
    prior service snapshot is runnable via `python versions/before/server.py` on
    port 8081. The current executable path is selected in `run.py`.
    

### checkout_app/__init__.py | sha256=e841164491c4e3eeedd963948f81818610dff8a08058792eeb99c559b31719c8
    """Checkout sample application."""
    

### checkout_app/__pycache__/__init__.cpython-311.pyc | sha256=0068a53b0b50af0b051e0ba44ab6d177133172da97eee38fb3dfbd56f6569db3

### checkout_app/__pycache__/checkout.cpython-311.pyc | sha256=5428cb87f61b6a5f464533e913678c14a95b689649337c0540cb5963daf415d2

### checkout_app/checkout.py | sha256=ad9b7786ee90545cbfd55c63c8e738b4af468d953ef134c95c3f25e16aa4e1a2
    """Current checkout behavior."""
    
    
    def create_checkout(cart, method="card"):
        items = cart.get("items", [])
        if not items or any(item.get("quantity", 0) < 1 for item in items):
            raise ValueError("The cart needs items with positive quantities")
        if method not in ("card", "bank_transfer"):
            raise ValueError("Unsupported payment method")
        cents = sum(item["price_cents"] * item["quantity"] for item in items)
        return {"status": "pending_payment", "amount_cents": cents, "method": method}
    

### checkout_app/server.py | sha256=44ce196b97a7fd4eb2d4ba14f6de214be2fe30d03628c7beae61d50ebea83992
    """Standard-library HTTP entry point; no external package installation."""
    import json
    from http.server import BaseHTTPRequestHandler, ThreadingHTTPServer
    from pathlib import Path
    from .checkout import create_checkout
    
    WEB_ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent / "web"
    
    
    class Handler(BaseHTTPRequestHandler):
        def do_GET(self):
            if self.path == "/api/status":
                return self.reply(200, {"service": "checkout", "version": "2.0"})
            if self.path in ("/", "/app.js"):
                name = "index.html" if self.path == "/" else "app.js"
                body = (WEB_ROOT / name).read_bytes()
                self.send_response(200)
                self.send_header("Content-Type", "text/html" if name.endswith("html") else "text/javascript")
                self.send_header("Content-Length", str(len(body)))
                self.end_headers()
                self.wfile.write(body)
                return
            self.reply(404, {"error": "Not found"})
    
        def do_POST(self):
            if self.path != "/api/checkout":
                return self.reply(404, {"error": "Not found"})
            try:
                size = int(self.headers.get("Content-Length", "0"))
                if size > 65536:
                    raise ValueError("Request too large")
                payload = json.loads(self.rfile.read(size))
                result = create_checkout(payload["cart"], payload.get("method", "card"))
                self.reply(200, result)
            except (KeyError, ValueError, TypeError, json.JSONDecodeError) as exc:
                self.reply(400, {"error": str(exc)})
    
        def reply(self, status, value):
            body = json.dumps(value).encode()
            self.send_response(status)
            self.send_header("Content-Type", "application/json")
            self.send_header("Content-Length", str(len(body)))
            self.end_headers()
            self.wfile.write(body)
    
    
    def main():
        ThreadingHTTPServer(("127.0.0.1", 8080), Handler).serve_forever()
    
    
    if __name__ == "__main__":
        main()

### legacy_compat.py | sha256=547b4b2a1ed3f5b5890fa08a47e949c1f97bd0cd58985b21a23798f9b193b470
    """Old v1 HTTP path, retained temporarily across the upgrade."""
    from http.server import BaseHTTPRequestHandler, ThreadingHTTPServer
    
    
    class LegacyHandler(BaseHTTPRequestHandler):
        def do_POST(self):
            self.send_response(410)
            self.end_headers()
            self.wfile.write(b"Legacy checkout retired")
    
    
    def main():
        ThreadingHTTPServer(("127.0.0.1", 8080), LegacyHandler).serve_forever()

    """Service launcher for the current release."""
    from checkout_app.server import main as current_main
    
    
    def main():
        current_main()
    
    
    if __name__ == "__main__":
        main()
    

### tests/__pycache__/test_checkout.cpython-311.pyc | sha256=3334d6531a86021acef58ba1cdde12c5795af08b6957e59c35e54f1ba4a9b2dd

### tests/test_checkout.py | sha256=8c7e2584b3ab04fbafe603d87063c839346f5719e5f74fc36b43d6e5166a6ffe
    import unittest
    from checkout_app.checkout import create_checkout
    
    
    class CheckoutTests(unittest.TestCase):
        def test_card_checkout(self):
            self.assertEqual(create_checkout({"items": [{"price_cents": 1250, "quantity": 2}]}),
                             {"status": "pending_payment", "amount_cents": 2500, "method": "card"})
    
        def test_empty_cart(self):
            with self.assertRaises(ValueError):
                create_checkout({"items": []})
    
    
    if __name__ == "__main__":
        unittest.main()

### versions/before/server.py | sha256=c18b186ec348b26ed30cffffd04d688f3661f4abc18260a851f307bad09e6e56
    """Runnable checkout v1.9 snapshot from before the upgrade.
    
    Run `python versions/before/server.py` in the project root. This older service
    listens on 8081 so its behavior can be compared with the new service on 8080.
    """
    import json
    from http.server import BaseHTTPRequestHandler, ThreadingHTTPServer
    
    
    class Handler(BaseHTTPRequestHandler):
        def do_GET(self):
            if self.path == "/api/status":
                return self.reply(200, {"service": "checkout", "version": "1.9"})
            self.reply(404, {"error": "Not found"})
    
        def do_POST(self):
            if self.path != "/api/checkout":
                return self.reply(404, {"error": "Not found"})
            try:
                payload = json.loads(self.rfile.read(int(self.headers.get("Content-Length", "0"))))
                items = payload["cart"]["items"]
                if not items:
                    raise ValueError("Cart is empty")
                amount = sum(item["price_cents"] * item["quantity"] for item in items)
                self.reply(200, {"status": "pending_payment", "amount_cents": amount, "method": "card"})
            except (KeyError, ValueError, TypeError) as exc:
                self.reply(400, {"error": str(exc)})
    
        def reply(self, status, value):
            body = json.dumps(value).encode()
            self.send_response(status)
            self.send_header("Content-Type", "application/json")
            self.send_header("Content-Length", str(len(body)))
            self.end_headers()
            self.wfile.write(body)
    
    
    if __name__ == "__main__":
        ThreadingHTTPServer(("127.0.0.1", 8081), Handler).serve_forever()
    

### web/app.js | sha256=ea71ea811176430b89c4075881c3381cac04be21b3aeeb105d7ce12e5821f8c3
    fetch("/api/status")
      .then(response => response.json())
      .then(status => { document.querySelector("#status").textContent = `Checkout ${status.version}`; });
